2026-10-18T15:51:41.3183402Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] Observe the EAGLE3 per-verify acceptance distribution with 6 draft steps

Purpose: measure, for every verify step, how many draft tokens the
target accepts when the draft proposes 6 tokens instead of 3. This is an
observation run only; the user accepts that it is slower and scores
lower than main (7999.37).

Changes on top of main a19f971 (experiment branch, not for merge):
- configs/firmus-framework.json: speculation steps 3 -> 6, draft tokens
  4 -> 7. SGLang fc275674 requires draft_tokens == steps + 1 for topk 1
  (speculative/base_spec_worker.py:58), so 6 proposed tokens need 7
  verify tokens; provisioning checks the same relation.
- patches/sglang/qwen3-sm90-megamoe.patch: the Decode guard accepted only
  speculative_num_draft_tokens == 4; allow 4 or 7. No kernel change.
- sglang.sbatch: the Decode worker logs every finished request as JSON
  (--log-requests --log-requests-level 0 --log-requests-format json
  --log-requests-target stdout) into decode-0.log. SGLang already keeps a
  per-request spec_correct_drafts_histogram (count of verify steps that
  accepted k = 0..6 drafts) and spec_verify_ct in meta_info.
- tests: read the speculation settings from the config instead of
  pinning 3 / 4.

Expected side effects: with 32 requests x 7 verify tokens = 224 tokens
per Decode rank, Decode MegaMoE leaves SwapAB (limit 128 tokens) for the
BLOCK_M 64 non-SwapAB path, and the draft runs 6 sequential steps, so ITL
rises and ITL failures may appear. Decode cap stays 128 so the number of
observed requests is comparable. No profile pass.
